"""
Generic read-only discovery for dynamic application analysis.

Discovery observes an authorised HTTP(S) application and builds a
structured representation of its reachable attack surface.

This module does NOT:

- decide whether a vulnerability exists;
- generate vulnerability findings;
- execute attack payloads;
- submit discovered forms;
- depend on DVWA, Juice Shop, or another specific application.

Architecture principle:

    DynamicTarget
        ->
    Read-only discovery
        ->
    Dynamic context
        ->
    Candidate proposals
        ->
    Evidence verifies
        ->
    Human decides
"""

from __future__ import annotations

from dataclasses import dataclass, field
from typing import Any
from urllib.parse import (
    parse_qsl,
    urldefrag,
    urljoin,
    urlsplit,
    urlunsplit,
)

import requests
from bs4 import BeautifulSoup

from app.dynamic_analysis.target import DynamicTarget


DEFAULT_REQUEST_TIMEOUT = 10
DEFAULT_MAX_PAGES = 10
DEFAULT_MAX_RESPONSE_BYTES = 1_000_000
DEFAULT_MAX_RESPONSE_EXCERPT_CHARS = 2_000

DISCOVERY_USER_AGENT = "Evidence-Gated-AppSec/0.1 Discovery"


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class DiscoveredParameter:
    """One observable input parameter."""

    name: str
    location: str
    input_type: str | None = None
    observed_value: str | None = None

    def to_dict(self) -> dict[str, Any]:
        return {
            "name": self.name,
            "location": self.location,
            "input_type": self.input_type,
            "observed_value": self.observed_value,
        }


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class DiscoveredForm:
    """One HTML form observed during discovery."""

    source_url: str
    action_url: str
    method: str
    parameters: tuple[DiscoveredParameter, ...]

    def to_dict(self) -> dict[str, Any]:
        return {
            "source_url": self.source_url,
            "action_url": self.action_url,
            "method": self.method,
            "parameters": [
                parameter.to_dict()
                for parameter in self.parameters
            ],
        }


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class DiscoveredEndpoint:
    """One HTTP endpoint observed during discovery."""

    url: str
    method: str
    parameters: tuple[DiscoveredParameter, ...] = ()

    def to_dict(self) -> dict[str, Any]:
        return {
            "url": self.url,
            "method": self.method,
            "parameters": [
                parameter.to_dict()
                for parameter in self.parameters
            ],
        }


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class DiscoveredPage:
    """One page fetched successfully enough to inspect."""

    requested_url: str
    final_url: str
    status_code: int
    content_type: str | None
    response_length: int
    title: str | None
    response_header_names: tuple[str, ...] = ()
    cookie_names: tuple[str, ...] = ()
    redirect_count: int = 0
    # Small, bounded textual sample of the already-fetched response.
    # This is observation only; it is never treated as verification evidence.
    response_excerpt: str | None = None

    def to_dict(self) -> dict[str, Any]:
        return {
            "requested_url": self.requested_url,
            "final_url": self.final_url,
            "status_code": self.status_code,
            "content_type": self.content_type,
            "response_length": self.response_length,
            "title": self.title,
            "response_header_names": list(
                self.response_header_names
            ),
            "cookie_names": list(
                self.cookie_names
            ),
            "redirect_count": self.redirect_count,
            "response_excerpt": self.response_excerpt,
        }


@dataclass
class DiscoveryResult:
    """Structured output produced by read-only discovery."""

    target: DynamicTarget
    pages: list[DiscoveredPage] = field(default_factory=list)
    endpoints: list[DiscoveredEndpoint] = field(default_factory=list)
    forms: list[DiscoveredForm] = field(default_factory=list)
    external_links: list[str] = field(default_factory=list)
    errors: list[dict[str, str]] = field(default_factory=list)

    @property
    def parameters(self) -> list[DiscoveredParameter]:
        """
        Return unique parameters observed across endpoints and forms.
        """

        unique: dict[
            tuple[str, str, str | None],
            DiscoveredParameter,
        ] = {}

        for endpoint in self.endpoints:
            for parameter in endpoint.parameters:
                key = (
                    parameter.name,
                    parameter.location,
                    parameter.input_type,
                )
                unique[key] = parameter

        for form in self.forms:
            for parameter in form.parameters:
                key = (
                    parameter.name,
                    parameter.location,
                    parameter.input_type,
                )
                unique[key] = parameter

        return list(unique.values())

    def to_dict(self) -> dict[str, Any]:
        return {
            "target": {
                "original_url": self.target.original_url,
                "base_url": self.target.base_url,
                "origin": self.target.origin,
                "scheme": self.target.scheme,
                "hostname": self.target.hostname,
                "port": self.target.port,
                "start_path": self.target.start_path,
            },
            "pages": [
                page.to_dict()
                for page in self.pages
            ],
            "endpoints": [
                endpoint.to_dict()
                for endpoint in self.endpoints
            ],
            "forms": [
                form.to_dict()
                for form in self.forms
            ],
            "parameters": [
                parameter.to_dict()
                for parameter in self.parameters
            ],
            "external_links": list(self.external_links),
            "errors": list(self.errors),
            "summary": {
                "pages_discovered": len(self.pages),
                "endpoints_discovered": len(self.endpoints),
                "forms_discovered": len(self.forms),
                "parameters_discovered": len(self.parameters),
                "external_links_observed": len(self.external_links),
                "errors": len(self.errors),
            },
        }


class DynamicDiscovery:
    """
    Bounded same-origin crawler for generic application discovery.

    Only GET requests are performed.

    Forms are observed but never submitted by this component.
    """

    def __init__(
        self,
        *,
        session: requests.Session | None = None,
        request_timeout: int = DEFAULT_REQUEST_TIMEOUT,
        max_pages: int = DEFAULT_MAX_PAGES,
        max_response_bytes: int = DEFAULT_MAX_RESPONSE_BYTES,
        max_response_excerpt_chars: int = DEFAULT_MAX_RESPONSE_EXCERPT_CHARS,
    ) -> None:
        if request_timeout <= 0:
            raise ValueError(
                "request_timeout must be greater than zero."
            )

        if max_pages <= 0:
            raise ValueError(
                "max_pages must be greater than zero."
            )

        if max_response_bytes <= 0:
            raise ValueError(
                "max_response_bytes must be greater than zero."
            )

        if max_response_excerpt_chars <= 0:
            raise ValueError(
                "max_response_excerpt_chars must be greater than zero."
            )

        self.session = session or requests.Session()

        self.session.headers.update({
            "User-Agent": DISCOVERY_USER_AGENT,
        })

        self.request_timeout = request_timeout
        self.max_pages = max_pages
        self.max_response_bytes = max_response_bytes
        self.max_response_excerpt_chars = max_response_excerpt_chars

    def discover(
        self,
        target: DynamicTarget,
    ) -> DiscoveryResult:
        """Discover a bounded same-origin application surface."""

        if not isinstance(target, DynamicTarget):
            raise TypeError(
                "target must be a DynamicTarget."
            )

        result = DiscoveryResult(
            target=target,
        )

        queue = [
            _normalise_discovery_url(
                target.base_url
            )
        ]

        queued = set(queue)
        visited: set[str] = set()

        endpoint_keys: set[
            tuple[str, str, tuple[tuple[str, str], ...]]
        ] = set()

        form_keys: set[
            tuple[str, str, tuple[str, ...]]
        ] = set()

        external_seen: set[str] = set()

        while queue and len(visited) < self.max_pages:
            current_url = queue.pop(0)
            queued.discard(current_url)

            if current_url in visited:
                continue

            visited.add(current_url)

            try:
                response = self.session.get(
                    current_url,
                    timeout=self.request_timeout,
                    allow_redirects=True,
                )

            except requests.RequestException as exc:
                result.errors.append({
                    "url": current_url,
                    "error": str(exc),
                })
                continue

            final_url = _normalise_discovery_url(
                response.url
            )

            # A redirect may leave the authorised origin.
            # Record the observation, but do not inspect/crawl it.
            if not target.is_same_origin(final_url):
                if final_url not in external_seen:
                    result.external_links.append(
                        final_url
                    )
                    external_seen.add(final_url)

                continue

            content_type = response.headers.get(
                "Content-Type"
            )

            response_length = len(
                response.content
            )

            title = None

            is_html = _is_html_response(
                content_type
            )

            within_body_budget = (
                response_length
                <= self.max_response_bytes
            )

            soup = None

            if is_html and within_body_budget:
                soup = BeautifulSoup(
                    response.text,
                    "html.parser",
                )

                if soup.title:
                    title_text = soup.title.get_text(
                        strip=True
                    )

                    if title_text:
                        title = title_text

            response_header_names = tuple(
                sorted(
                    {
                        str(name).strip()
                        for name in response.headers.keys()
                        if str(name).strip()
                    },
                    key=str.lower,
                )
            )

            cookie_names = tuple(
                sorted(
                    {
                        str(cookie.name).strip()
                        for cookie in response.cookies
                        if str(cookie.name).strip()
                    },
                    key=str.lower,
                )
            )

            redirect_count = len(
                getattr(
                    response,
                    "history",
                    (),
                )
            )

            response_excerpt = _build_response_excerpt(
                response=response,
                content_type=content_type,
                within_body_budget=within_body_budget,
                max_chars=self.max_response_excerpt_chars,
            )

            result.pages.append(
                DiscoveredPage(
                    requested_url=current_url,
                    final_url=final_url,
                    status_code=response.status_code,
                    content_type=content_type,
                    response_length=response_length,
                    title=title,
                    response_header_names=(
                        response_header_names
                    ),
                    cookie_names=cookie_names,
                    redirect_count=redirect_count,
                    response_excerpt=response_excerpt,
                )
            )

            query_parameters = (
                _extract_query_parameters(
                    final_url
                )
            )

            _append_endpoint(
                result=result,
                endpoint_keys=endpoint_keys,
                endpoint=DiscoveredEndpoint(
                    url=final_url,
                    method="GET",
                    parameters=tuple(
                        query_parameters
                    ),
                ),
            )

            if soup is None:
                continue

            for anchor in soup.find_all(
                "a",
                href=True,
            ):
                href = str(
                    anchor.get("href", "")
                ).strip()

                resolved = _resolve_http_url(
                    base_url=final_url,
                    candidate=href,
                )

                if resolved is None:
                    continue

                if target.is_same_origin(resolved):
                    link_parameters = (
                        _extract_query_parameters(
                            resolved
                        )
                    )

                    _append_endpoint(
                        result=result,
                        endpoint_keys=endpoint_keys,
                        endpoint=DiscoveredEndpoint(
                            url=resolved,
                            method="GET",
                            parameters=tuple(
                                link_parameters
                            ),
                        ),
                    )

                    crawl_url = (
                        _normalise_discovery_url(
                            resolved
                        )
                    )

                    if (
                        crawl_url not in visited
                        and crawl_url not in queued
                    ):
                        queue.append(
                            crawl_url
                        )
                        queued.add(
                            crawl_url
                        )

                elif resolved not in external_seen:
                    result.external_links.append(
                        resolved
                    )
                    external_seen.add(
                        resolved
                    )

            for form_element in soup.find_all(
                "form"
            ):
                form = _parse_form(
                    source_url=final_url,
                    form_element=form_element,
                )

                if not target.is_same_origin(
                    form.action_url
                ):
                    if (
                        form.action_url
                        not in external_seen
                    ):
                        result.external_links.append(
                            form.action_url
                        )
                        external_seen.add(
                            form.action_url
                        )

                    continue

                form_key = (
                    form.action_url,
                    form.method,
                    tuple(
                        parameter.name
                        for parameter in form.parameters
                    ),
                )

                if form_key not in form_keys:
                    result.forms.append(
                        form
                    )
                    form_keys.add(
                        form_key
                    )

                _append_endpoint(
                    result=result,
                    endpoint_keys=endpoint_keys,
                    endpoint=DiscoveredEndpoint(
                        url=form.action_url,
                        method=form.method,
                        parameters=form.parameters,
                    ),
                )

        return result


def _parse_form(
    *,
    source_url: str,
    form_element: Any,
) -> DiscoveredForm:
    """Convert an HTML form into a structured observation."""

    raw_action = str(
        form_element.get(
            "action",
            "",
        )
    ).strip()

    action_url = (
        _resolve_http_url(
            base_url=source_url,
            candidate=raw_action,
        )
        or source_url
    )

    raw_method = str(
        form_element.get(
            "method",
            "GET",
        )
    ).strip().upper()

    method = (
        raw_method
        if raw_method in {"GET", "POST"}
        else "GET"
    )

    parameter_location = (
        "query"
        if method == "GET"
        else "body"
    )

    parameters: list[
        DiscoveredParameter
    ] = []

    seen_names: set[str] = set()

    field_elements = form_element.find_all(
        ["input", "textarea", "select", "button"]
    )

    for field_element in field_elements:
        raw_name = field_element.get(
            "name"
        )

        if raw_name is None:
            continue

        name = str(raw_name).strip()

        if not name or name in seen_names:
            continue

        input_type = (
            str(
                field_element.get(
                    "type",
                    field_element.name,
                )
            )
            .strip()
            .lower()
        )

        observed_value = _extract_form_control_value(
            field_element
        )

        parameters.append(
            DiscoveredParameter(
                name=name,
                location=parameter_location,
                input_type=input_type,
                observed_value=observed_value,
            )
        )

        seen_names.add(
            name
        )

    return DiscoveredForm(
        source_url=source_url,
        action_url=_normalise_discovery_url(
            action_url
        ),
        method=method,
        parameters=tuple(parameters),
    )



def _extract_form_control_value(
    field_element: Any,
) -> str | None:
    """Return a reproducible default value for an observed HTML control.

    This is application-neutral discovery metadata. It does not submit the
    form, infer a vulnerability, or generate attack values.
    """

    tag_name = str(
        getattr(field_element, "name", "")
    ).strip().lower()

    input_type = str(
        field_element.get(
            "type",
            tag_name,
        )
    ).strip().lower()

    if tag_name == "textarea":
        return field_element.get_text()

    if tag_name == "select":
        selected = field_element.find(
            "option",
            selected=True,
        )

        if selected is None:
            selected = field_element.find("option")

        if selected is None:
            return None

        raw_value = selected.get("value")

        if raw_value is not None:
            return str(raw_value)

        return selected.get_text()

    if input_type in {"checkbox", "radio"}:
        if not field_element.has_attr("checked"):
            return None

        raw_value = field_element.get("value")

        return (
            str(raw_value)
            if raw_value is not None
            else "on"
        )

    if input_type == "file":
        return None

    raw_value = field_element.get("value")

    if raw_value is not None:
        return str(raw_value)

    # Text-like controls are successful controls even when their browser
    # default is the empty string. Submit/button controls without an explicit
    # value are also represented as empty rather than inventing a label.
    if tag_name in {"input", "button"}:
        return ""

    return None

def _extract_query_parameters(
    url: str,
) -> list[DiscoveredParameter]:
    """Extract unique query parameter names from a URL."""

    parsed = urlsplit(
        url
    )

    parameters: list[
        DiscoveredParameter
    ] = []

    seen: set[str] = set()

    for name, value in parse_qsl(
        parsed.query,
        keep_blank_values=True,
    ):
        if not name or name in seen:
            continue

        parameters.append(
            DiscoveredParameter(
                name=name,
                location="query",
                input_type=None,
                observed_value=value,
            )
        )

        seen.add(
            name
        )

    return parameters


def _resolve_http_url(
    *,
    base_url: str,
    candidate: str,
) -> str | None:
    """Resolve an HTTP(S) link and discard unsupported URL schemes."""

    candidate = candidate.strip()

    if not candidate:
        return _normalise_discovery_url(
            base_url
        )

    lowered = candidate.lower()

    if lowered.startswith(
        (
            "javascript:",
            "mailto:",
            "tel:",
            "data:",
        )
    ):
        return None

    resolved = urljoin(
        base_url,
        candidate,
    )

    resolved, _ = urldefrag(
        resolved
    )

    parsed = urlsplit(
        resolved
    )

    if parsed.scheme.lower() not in {
        "http",
        "https",
    }:
        return None

    return _normalise_discovery_url(
        resolved
    )


def _normalise_discovery_url(
    url: str,
) -> str:
    """Remove fragments while preserving path and query information."""

    clean_url, _ = urldefrag(
        url
    )

    parsed = urlsplit(
        clean_url
    )

    path = parsed.path or "/"

    return urlunsplit(
        (
            parsed.scheme.lower(),
            parsed.netloc,
            path,
            parsed.query,
            "",
        )
    )


def _build_response_excerpt(
    *,
    response: requests.Response,
    content_type: str | None,
    within_body_budget: bool,
    max_chars: int,
) -> str | None:
    """Return a bounded textual sample of an already-fetched response.

    No additional request is made. Binary/oversized responses are ignored.
    HTML is reduced to visible text so hidden fields, scripts and markup are
    not forwarded merely for proposal generation. JSON/text responses retain
    their textual representation.
    """

    if not within_body_budget:
        return None

    lowered = str(content_type or "").lower()
    textual = (
        not lowered
        or lowered.startswith("text/")
        or "json" in lowered
        or "xml" in lowered
        or "javascript" in lowered
        or "x-www-form-urlencoded" in lowered
    )
    if not textual:
        return None

    text = response.text or ""
    if _is_html_response(content_type):
        soup = BeautifulSoup(text, "html.parser")
        for element in soup(["script", "style", "noscript"]):
            element.decompose()
        text = soup.get_text(" ", strip=True)

    # Keep the excerpt compact and deterministic without changing content.
    text = " ".join(text.split())
    if not text:
        return None

    if len(text) <= max_chars:
        return text

    return text[:max_chars] + "â€¦"


def _is_html_response(
    content_type: str | None,
) -> bool:
    """Return True when a response appears to contain HTML."""

    if content_type is None:
        return False

    return "text/html" in content_type.lower()


def _append_endpoint(
    *,
    result: DiscoveryResult,
    endpoint_keys: set,
    endpoint: DiscoveredEndpoint,
) -> None:
    """Append an endpoint only when the observation is new."""

    key = (
        endpoint.url,
        endpoint.method,
        tuple(
            (
                parameter.name,
                parameter.location,
            )
            for parameter in endpoint.parameters
        ),
    )

    if key in endpoint_keys:
        return

    result.endpoints.append(
        endpoint
    )

    endpoint_keys.add(
        key
    )




============================================================
 4. CONTEXT_BUILDER.PY - COMPLETE FILE
============================================================

"""
Generic dynamic-analysis context builder.

Transforms deterministic discovery observations into bounded context
units suitable for downstream LLM analysis.

Responsibilities:

- preserve runtime observation provenance;
- keep endpoints and forms as meaningful analysis units;
- distinguish observed facts from future inferred metadata;
- enforce deterministic context budgets;
- report analysis coverage honestly.

This module does NOT:

- call an LLM;
- detect vulnerabilities;
- execute attack payloads;
- verify security findings;
- infer an application category.

Architecture principle:

    Dynamic discovery
        ->
    Dynamic context builder
        ->
    Bounded context units
        ->
    Selected LLM proposer
        ->
    Candidate proposals
        ->
    Evidence verifies
        ->
    Human decides
"""

from __future__ import annotations

from dataclasses import asdict, dataclass
from typing import Any

from app.dynamic_analysis.discovery import (
    DiscoveryResult,
    DiscoveredEndpoint,
    DiscoveredForm,
    DiscoveredPage,
)


# The discovery layer remains complete; this budget only limits how many
# deterministic context units are forwarded to the LLM in one analysis pass.
# A bounded default prevents local/remote providers from receiving dozens of
# sequential requests while coverage metadata still reports everything found.
DEFAULT_MAX_CONTEXTS = 12
DEFAULT_MAX_CONTEXT_CHARS = 40_000


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class DynamicContext:
    """
    One deterministic unit of runtime context.

    A context describes an observed endpoint or form. It contains facts
    collected during discovery, not vulnerability conclusions.
    """

    context_id: str
    context_type: str

    source_url: str
    endpoint_url: str
    method: str

    parameters: tuple[dict[str, Any], ...]

    observed_from: str

    context_text: str
    char_count: int

    # Optional enriched HTTP metadata. Keeping this field optional preserves
    # compatibility with existing callers/tests that construct DynamicContext
    # without HTTP response observations.
    http_observation: dict[str, Any] | None = None

    def to_dict(self) -> dict[str, Any]:
        return asdict(self)


@dataclass
class DynamicContextBuildResult:
    """Dynamic contexts plus transparent coverage information."""

    contexts: list[DynamicContext]

    endpoints_discovered: int
    forms_discovered: int

    contexts_created: int
    contexts_selected: int
    contexts_excluded_by_budget: int

    total_context_chars: int
    selected_chars: int

    truncated: bool
    truncation_reason: str | None

    def to_dict(self) -> dict[str, Any]:
        return {
            "contexts": [
                context.to_dict()
                for context in self.contexts
            ],
            "coverage": {
                "endpoints_discovered":
                    self.endpoints_discovered,
                "forms_discovered":
                    self.forms_discovered,
                "contexts_created":
                    self.contexts_created,
                "contexts_selected":
                    self.contexts_selected,
                "contexts_excluded_by_budget":
                    self.contexts_excluded_by_budget,
                "total_context_chars":
                    self.total_context_chars,
                "selected_chars":
                    self.selected_chars,
                "truncated":
                    self.truncated,
                "truncation_reason":
                    self.truncation_reason,
            },
        }


class DynamicContextBuilder:
    """
    Build deterministic LLM-ready context from discovery observations.

    Contexts are intentionally endpoint/form oriented rather than
    source-file oriented.

    Application-category inference is deliberately outside this class.
    A future classifier may enrich downstream analysis without changing
    the deterministic observations produced here.
    """

    def __init__(
        self,
        *,
        max_contexts: int = DEFAULT_MAX_CONTEXTS,
        max_context_chars: int = DEFAULT_MAX_CONTEXT_CHARS,
    ) -> None:
        if max_contexts <= 0:
            raise ValueError(
                "max_contexts must be greater than zero."
            )

        if max_context_chars <= 0:
            raise ValueError(
                "max_context_chars must be greater than zero."
            )

        self.max_contexts = max_contexts
        self.max_context_chars = max_context_chars

    def build(
        self,
        discovery: DiscoveryResult,
    ) -> DynamicContextBuildResult:
        """
        Convert discovery observations into bounded contexts.

        Forms are emitted first because they provide richer information
        about methods and input locations. Remaining endpoint
        observations are then emitted deterministically.
        """

        if not isinstance(
            discovery,
            DiscoveryResult,
        ):
            raise TypeError(
                "discovery must be a DiscoveryResult."
            )

        candidate_contexts = (
            self._build_candidate_contexts(
                discovery
            )
        )

        # Prioritisation is deliberately generic and deterministic. It uses
        # only runtime observations already collected by discovery; it does
        # not infer a vulnerability, inspect a target name, or special-case
        # any application. Rich input-bearing surfaces are considered first
        # because they give the proposer more useful observed context per LLM
        # call. All candidates still contribute to coverage metadata below.
        candidate_contexts = self._prioritise_contexts(
            candidate_contexts
        )

        total_context_chars = sum(
            context.char_count
            for context in candidate_contexts
        )

        selected_contexts: list[
            DynamicContext
        ] = []

        selected_chars = 0

        truncated = False
        truncation_reason: str | None = None

        for context in candidate_contexts:
            if (
                len(selected_contexts)
                >= self.max_contexts
            ):
                truncated = True
                truncation_reason = (
                    "Maximum number of dynamic contexts "
                    "selected for analysis was reached."
                )
                break

            if (
                selected_chars
                + context.char_count
                > self.max_context_chars
            ):
                truncated = True
                truncation_reason = (
                    "Maximum dynamic-context character "
                    "analysis budget was reached."
                )
                break

            selected_contexts.append(
                context
            )

            selected_chars += (
                context.char_count
            )

        contexts_created = len(
            candidate_contexts
        )

        contexts_selected = len(
            selected_contexts
        )

        contexts_excluded_by_budget = max(
            0,
            contexts_created
            - contexts_selected,
        )

        return DynamicContextBuildResult(
            contexts=selected_contexts,
            endpoints_discovered=len(
                discovery.endpoints
            ),
            forms_discovered=len(
                discovery.forms
            ),
            contexts_created=contexts_created,
            contexts_selected=contexts_selected,
            contexts_excluded_by_budget=(
                contexts_excluded_by_budget
            ),
            total_context_chars=(
                total_context_chars
            ),
            selected_chars=selected_chars,
            truncated=truncated,
            truncation_reason=(
                truncation_reason
            ),
        )

    def _build_candidate_contexts(
        self,
        discovery: DiscoveryResult,
    ) -> list[DynamicContext]:
        """
        Build deterministic contexts without duplicating form endpoints.

        A form contains richer information than its corresponding
        generic endpoint observation, so the form representation wins.
        """

        contexts: list[
            DynamicContext
        ] = []

        represented_endpoint_keys: set[
            tuple[str, str]
        ] = set()

        sorted_forms = sorted(
            discovery.forms,
            key=lambda form: (
                form.action_url.lower(),
                form.method,
                form.source_url.lower(),
            ),
        )

        for index, form in enumerate(
            sorted_forms,
            start=1,
        ):
            contexts.append(
                self._context_from_form(
                    form=form,
                    index=index,
                    page=_find_page_for_url(
                        discovery.pages,
                        form.source_url,
                    ),
                )
            )

            represented_endpoint_keys.add(
                (
                    form.action_url,
                    form.method,
                )
            )

        remaining_endpoints = sorted(
            discovery.endpoints,
            key=lambda endpoint: (
                endpoint.url.lower(),
                endpoint.method,
            ),
        )

        endpoint_index = 1

        for endpoint in remaining_endpoints:
            endpoint_key = (
                endpoint.url,
                endpoint.method,
            )

            if (
                endpoint_key
                in represented_endpoint_keys
            ):
                continue

            contexts.append(
                self._context_from_endpoint(
                    endpoint=endpoint,
                    index=endpoint_index,
                    page=_find_page_for_url(
                        discovery.pages,
                        endpoint.url,
                    ),
                )
            )

            endpoint_index += 1

        return contexts

    @staticmethod
    def _prioritise_contexts(
        contexts: list[DynamicContext],
    ) -> list[DynamicContext]:
        """
        Order contexts by generic observed analysis value.

        The ranking is intentionally vulnerability-agnostic and target-agnostic.
        It never searches for DVWA paths, CWE identifiers, SQL/XSS keywords, or
        expected findings. Stable original order is used as the final tie-breaker.
        """

        indexed_contexts = list(enumerate(contexts))

        def ranking(item: tuple[int, DynamicContext]) -> tuple[int, int, int, int, int]:
            original_index, context = item
            method = (context.method or "GET").upper()
            parameter_count = len(context.parameters)

            # Forms carry explicit input structure. Parameters are the next
            # strongest generic signal of user-controlled application surface.
            is_form = 1 if context.context_type.upper() == "FORM" else 0
            has_parameters = 1 if parameter_count else 0

            # Non-GET/HEAD methods often represent richer application actions.
            # This is only an ordering hint, never a vulnerability conclusion.
            stateful_method = 1 if method not in {"GET", "HEAD", "OPTIONS"} else 0

            has_http_observation = 1 if context.http_observation else 0

            return (
                -is_form,
                -has_parameters,
                -parameter_count,
                -stateful_method,
                -has_http_observation,
                original_index,
            )

        return [
            context
            for _, context in sorted(
                indexed_contexts,
                key=ranking,
            )
        ]

    @staticmethod
    def _context_from_form(
        *,
        form: DiscoveredForm,
        index: int,
        page: DiscoveredPage | None = None,
    ) -> DynamicContext:
        """Create context from an observed HTML form."""

        parameters = tuple(
            parameter.to_dict()
            for parameter in form.parameters
        )

        http_observation = _page_observation(
            page
        )

        context_text = (
            _render_context_text(
                context_type="FORM",
                source_url=form.source_url,
                endpoint_url=form.action_url,
                method=form.method,
                parameters=parameters,
                observed_from="HTML_FORM",
                http_observation=http_observation,
            )
        )

        return DynamicContext(
            context_id=(
                f"dynamic-form-{index:04d}"
            ),
            context_type="FORM",
            source_url=form.source_url,
            endpoint_url=form.action_url,
            method=form.method,
            parameters=parameters,
            observed_from="HTML_FORM",
            http_observation=http_observation,
            context_text=context_text,
            char_count=len(
                context_text
            ),
        )

    @staticmethod
    def _context_from_endpoint(
        *,
        endpoint: DiscoveredEndpoint,
        index: int,
        page: DiscoveredPage | None = None,
    ) -> DynamicContext:
        """Create context from an observed endpoint."""

        parameters = tuple(
            parameter.to_dict()
            for parameter in endpoint.parameters
        )

        http_observation = _page_observation(
            page
        )

        context_text = (
            _render_context_text(
                context_type="ENDPOINT",
                source_url=endpoint.url,
                endpoint_url=endpoint.url,
                method=endpoint.method,
                parameters=parameters,
                observed_from="DISCOVERY",
                http_observation=http_observation,
            )
        )

        return DynamicContext(
            context_id=(
                f"dynamic-endpoint-{index:04d}"
            ),
            context_type="ENDPOINT",
            source_url=endpoint.url,
            endpoint_url=endpoint.url,
            method=endpoint.method,
            parameters=parameters,
            observed_from="DISCOVERY",
            http_observation=http_observation,
            context_text=context_text,
            char_count=len(
                context_text
            ),
        )



def _find_page_for_url(
    pages: list[DiscoveredPage],
    url: str,
) -> DiscoveredPage | None:
    """Return the fetched page that exactly matches an observed URL."""

    for page in pages:
        if (
            page.final_url == url
            or page.requested_url == url
        ):
            return page

    return None


def _page_observation(
    page: DiscoveredPage | None,
) -> dict[str, Any] | None:
    """
    Convert fetched-page metadata into privacy-conscious LLM context.

    Header and cookie names are preserved, but values are not forwarded.
    """

    if page is None:
        return None

    return {
        "requested_url": page.requested_url,
        "final_url": page.final_url,
        "status_code": page.status_code,
        "content_type": page.content_type,
        "response_length": page.response_length,
        "title": page.title,
        "response_header_names": page.response_header_names,
        "cookie_names": page.cookie_names,
        "redirect_count": page.redirect_count,
        "response_excerpt": page.response_excerpt,
    }

def _render_context_text(
    *,
    context_type: str,
    source_url: str,
    endpoint_url: str,
    method: str,
    parameters: tuple[
        dict[str, Any],
        ...
    ],
    observed_from: str,
    http_observation: dict[str, Any] | None = None,
) -> str:
    """
    Render deterministic plain-text context for a downstream proposer.

    The wording intentionally distinguishes observations from security
    conclusions.
    """

    lines = [
        "DYNAMIC APPLICATION OBSERVATION",
        "",
        f"Context type: {context_type}",
        f"Observed from: {observed_from}",
        f"Source URL: {source_url}",
        f"Endpoint URL: {endpoint_url}",
        f"HTTP method: {method}",
        "",
        "Observed parameters:",
    ]

    if parameters:
        for parameter in parameters:
            name = parameter.get(
                "name",
                "",
            )

            location = parameter.get(
                "location",
                "unknown",
            )

            input_type = parameter.get(
                "input_type"
            )

            description = (
                f"- {name} "
                f"[location={location}"
            )

            if input_type:
                description += (
                    f", type={input_type}"
                )

            observed_value = parameter.get(
                "observed_value"
            )
            if observed_value is not None:
                value_text = str(observed_value)
                if len(value_text) > 120:
                    value_text = value_text[:120] + "â€¦"
                description += (
                    f", observed_value={value_text!r}"
                )

            description += "]"

            lines.append(
                description
            )

    else:
        lines.append(
            "- none observed"
        )

    lines.extend([
        "",
        "Observed HTTP response:",
    ])

    if http_observation:
        lines.extend([
            f"- Status code: {http_observation['status_code']}",
            (
                "- Content-Type: "
                f"{http_observation.get('content_type') or 'unknown'}"
            ),
            (
                "- Response length: "
                f"{http_observation['response_length']} bytes"
            ),
            (
                "- Page title: "
                f"{http_observation.get('title') or 'not observed'}"
            ),
            (
                "- Redirect count: "
                f"{http_observation['redirect_count']}"
            ),
        ])

        header_names = http_observation.get(
            "response_header_names",
            (),
        )
        lines.append(
            "- Response header names: "
            + (
                ", ".join(header_names)
                if header_names
                else "none observed"
            )
        )

        cookie_names = http_observation.get(
            "cookie_names",
            (),
        )
        lines.append(
            "- Cookie names: "
            + (
                ", ".join(cookie_names)
                if cookie_names
                else "none observed"
            )
        )

        response_excerpt = http_observation.get(
            "response_excerpt"
        )
        if response_excerpt:
            lines.extend([
                "",
                "Bounded response excerpt (observed, not verified):",
                str(response_excerpt),
            ])
    else:
        lines.append(
            "- no fetched response associated with this context"
        )

    lines.extend([
        "",
        (
            "Security status: "
            "NOT VERIFIED"
        ),
        (
            "This context contains observations only. "
            "It is not evidence that a vulnerability exists."
        ),
    ])

    return "\n".join(
        lines
    )

+def _build_response_excerpt(
+    *,
+    response: requests.Response,
+    content_type: str | None,
+    within_body_budget: bool,
+    max_chars: int,
+) -> str | None:
+    """Return a bounded textual sample of an already-fetched response.
+
+    No additional request is made. Binary/oversized responses are ignored.
+    HTML is reduced to visible text so hidden fields, scripts and markup are
+    not forwarded merely for proposal generation. JSON/text responses retain
+    their textual representation.
+    """
+
+    if not within_body_budget:
+        return None
+
+    lowered = str(content_type or "").lower()
+    textual = (
+        not lowered
+        or lowered.startswith("text/")
+        or "json" in lowered
+        or "xml" in lowered
+        or "javascript" in lowered
+        or "x-www-form-urlencoded" in lowered
+    )
+    if not textual:
+        return None
+
+    text = response.text or ""
+    if _is_html_response(content_type):
+        soup = BeautifulSoup(text, "html.parser")
+        for element in soup(["script", "style", "noscript"]):
+            element.decompose()
+        text = soup.get_text(" ", strip=True)
+
+    # Keep the excerpt compact and deterministic without changing content.
+    text = " ".join(text.split())
+    if not text:
+        return None
+
+    if len(text) <= max_chars:
+        return text
+
+    return text[:max_chars] + "ΓÇª"
+
+
 def _is_html_response(
     content_type: str | None,
 ) -> bool:

+    )

+# ======================================================================
+# ANALYSIS REPORTS / SAVED ANALYSES
+# ======================================================================
+
+SAVED_ANALYSES_DIR = Path("artifacts") / "saved_analyses"
+
+
+def _report_findings_for_analysis(analysis_id: str) -> list[dict]:
+    """Return the current in-memory findings that belong to one analysis."""
+    return [
+        build_view_model(finding)
+        for finding in FINDINGS.values()
+        if FINDING_METADATA.get(finding.finding_id, {}).get("analysis_id") == analysis_id
+    ]
+
+
+def _report_summary(findings: list[dict]) -> dict:
+    decisions = {"ACCEPTED": 0, "REJECTED": 0, "NEEDS_FURTHER_REVIEW": 0, "PENDING": 0}
+    gates = {"PROVED": 0, "REFUTED": 0, "NEEDS_HUMAN": 0, "PENDING": 0}
+    severities = {"LOW": 0, "MEDIUM": 0, "HIGH": 0, "CRITICAL": 0, "UNSET": 0}
+
+    for finding in findings:
+        decision = finding.get("human_decision") or "PENDING"
+        decisions[decision] = decisions.get(decision, 0) + 1
+
+        gate = finding.get("evidence_gate_result") or "PENDING"
+        gates[gate] = gates.get(gate, 0) + 1
+
+        severity = finding.get("final_severity") or finding.get("suggested_severity") or "UNSET"
+        severity = str(severity).upper()
+        severities[severity] = severities.get(severity, 0) + 1
+
+    return {
+        "total_findings": len(findings),
+        "decisions": decisions,
+        "verification": gates,
+        "severities": severities,
+    }
+
+
+def _build_analysis_report(analysis_id: str) -> dict:
+    analysis = ANALYSES.get(analysis_id)
+    if analysis is None:
+        abort(404)
+
+    findings = _report_findings_for_analysis(analysis_id)
+    return {
+        "schema_version": 1,
+        "analysis_id": analysis_id,
+        "generated_at_utc": datetime.now(timezone.utc).isoformat(),
+        "analysis": analysis,
+        "summary": _report_summary(findings),
+        "findings": findings,
+    }
+
+
+def _saved_analysis_path(analysis_id: str) -> Path:
+    safe_id = "".join(ch for ch in analysis_id if ch.isalnum() or ch in {"-", "_"})
+    if not safe_id or safe_id != analysis_id:
+        abort(400)
+    return SAVED_ANALYSES_DIR / f"{safe_id}.json"
+
+
+def _load_saved_analysis(analysis_id: str) -> dict | None:
+    path = _saved_analysis_path(analysis_id)
+    if not path.is_file():
+        return None
+    try:
+        return json.loads(path.read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
+    except (OSError, json.JSONDecodeError):
+        return None
+
+
+def _saved_analysis_cards() -> list[dict]:
+    if not SAVED_ANALYSES_DIR.is_dir():
+        return []
+
+    cards = []
+    for path in SAVED_ANALYSES_DIR.glob("*.json"):
+        try:
+            report = json.loads(path.read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
+        except (OSError, json.JSONDecodeError):
+            continue
+
+        analysis = report.get("analysis", {})
+        summary = report.get("summary", {})
+        cards.append({
+            "analysis_id": report.get("analysis_id", path.stem),
+            "saved_at_utc": report.get("saved_at_utc") or report.get("generated_at_utc"),
+            "analysis_type": analysis.get("analysis_type") or analysis.get("scan_type") or "UNKNOWN",
+            "provider": analysis.get("proposal_provider") or analysis.get("provider") or "UNKNOWN",
+            "target": analysis.get("target") or analysis.get("application_name") or analysis.get("source_file") or "Not available",
+            "authentication_mode": analysis.get("authentication_mode") or "ΓÇö",
+            "finding_count": summary.get("total_findings", len(report.get("findings", []))),
+        })
+
+    cards.sort(key=lambda item: item.get("saved_at_utc") or "", reverse=True)
+    return cards
+
+
+def _report_csv_text(report: dict) -> str:
+    from io import StringIO
+
+    output = StringIO()
+    fields = [
+        "analysis_id", "finding_id", "finding_type", "source_file", "line_number",
+        "chunk_start_line", "chunk_end_line", "endpoint", "parameter", "method",
+        "proposed_cwe", "llm_status", "suggested_severity", "verification_status",
+        "evidence_gate_result", "final_severity", "human_decision", "reviewer_comment",
+        "run_id", "evidence_source", "evidence_summary",
+    ]
+    writer = csv.DictWriter(output, fieldnames=fields)
+    writer.writeheader()
+
+    for finding in report.get("findings", []):
+        writer.writerow({field: finding.get(field) for field in fields})
+
+    return output.getvalue()
+
+
+@app.route("/analysis/<analysis_id>/report")
+def analysis_report(analysis_id):
+    # A saved snapshot remains viewable even after a later scan replaces the
+    # active in-memory finding set. The active analysis is otherwise rendered live.
+    saved = _load_saved_analysis(analysis_id)
+    if saved is not None and analysis_id not in ANALYSES:
+        report = saved
+        is_saved = True
+    else:
+        report = _build_analysis_report(analysis_id)
+        is_saved = _saved_analysis_path(analysis_id).is_file()
+
+    return render_template(
+        "analysis_report.html",
+        report=report,
+        is_saved=is_saved,
+        active_page="reports",
+    )
+
+
+@app.route("/analysis/<analysis_id>/save", methods=["POST"])
+def save_analysis(analysis_id):
+    report = _build_analysis_report(analysis_id)
+    report["saved_at_utc"] = datetime.now(timezone.utc).isoformat()
+
+    SAVED_ANALYSES_DIR.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
+    path = _saved_analysis_path(analysis_id)
+    path.write_text(json.dumps(report, indent=2, default=str), encoding="utf-8")
+
+    return jsonify({
+        "ok": True,
+        "analysis_id": analysis_id,
+        "saved": True,
+        "report_url": url_for("analysis_report", analysis_id=analysis_id),
+    })
+
+
+@app.route("/reports")
+def reports_list():
+    return render_template(
+        "reports.html",
+        saved_analyses=_saved_analysis_cards(),
+        active_page="reports",
+    )
+
+
+@app.route("/reports/<analysis_id>")
+def saved_report(analysis_id):
+    report = _load_saved_analysis(analysis_id)
+    if report is None:
+        abort(404)
+    return render_template(
+        "analysis_report.html",
+        report=report,
+        is_saved=True,
+        active_page="reports",
+    )
+
+
+@app.route("/reports/<analysis_id>/export/<format_name>")
+def export_report(analysis_id, format_name):
+    report = _load_saved_analysis(analysis_id)
+    if report is None:
+        # Allow preview/export of the active analysis before explicitly saving it.
+        report = _build_analysis_report(analysis_id)
+
+    format_name = format_name.lower()
+    if format_name == "json":
+        body = json.dumps(report, indent=2, default=str)
+        mimetype = "application/json"
+        extension = "json"
+    elif format_name == "csv":
+        body = _report_csv_text(report)
+        mimetype = "text/csv"
+        extension = "csv"
+    else:
+        abort(404)
+
+    return app.response_class(
+        body,
+        mimetype=mimetype,
+        headers={
+            "Content-Disposition": f'attachment; filename="{analysis_id}_report.{extension}"'
+        },
+    )
+
+

 # ======================================================================

     app.run(
         debug=True
     )
